CORPORATE PROFILE
公司概況
China Foods Limited (“China Foods” or the “Company” and together with its subsidiaries,
the “Group”), a subsidiary of COFCO Corporation (“COFCO”), is listed on the main board
of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (Stock Code: 506).


The Company mainly manufactures, distributes, markets and sells the Coca-Cola series
products at nineteen provincial-level administrative regions in the PRC through COFCO
Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“COFCO Coca-Cola”), a 65%-owned joint-venture with The
Coca-Cola Company. COFCO Coca-Cola offers twenty-five brands of products in ten major
types of beverages which include sparkling drink, juice, water, milk drink, energy drink,
tea, coffee, functional nutrition drink, sports drink and plant-based protein drink. The
Company also through its subsidiaries operates innovative business.


中國食品有限公司（簡稱「中國食品」或「本公司」，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）為中
糧集團有限公司（「中糧」）的附屬公司，並於香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市（股份代
號：506）。


本公司目前主要通過與可口可樂公司合作成立並持有65%權益的中糧可口可樂飲料有
限公司（簡稱「中糧可口可樂」）在國內19個省級行政區域從事生產、配送、推廣和銷售
可口可樂系列產品，向消費者提供包括汽水、果汁、水、乳飲料、能量飲料、茶、咖
啡、功能型營養素飲料、運動飲料及植物蛋白飲料等10大品類、25個品牌的產品。本
公司也透過旗下附屬公司從事創新業務。

On 14 April, COFCO Coca-Cola’s 25th anniversary series of events were held at COFCO Plaza. Members of the Standing
Committee of the Party Committee of the Group attended the events, with Li Guoqiang, Secretary of the Standing
Committee of the Party Committee and Chairman of COFCO, delivering a speech.

長李國強致辭。

On 20 April, LOHAS partnered with Beijing Half Marathon as the official water sponsor, launching its sports scene
marketing campaign. Breaking with tradition, LOHAS sponsored both natural mineral water and soda water, inviting
world champions to lead the LOHAS Running Club. Through deep collaboration with vertical media and influencers,
LOHAS achieved brand communication and category promotion.

4月20日，悅活合作北京半程馬拉松，以國馬官方飲用水正式開啟運動場景營銷。打破傳統以天然礦泉水與蘇打水
雙水贊助，邀請世界冠軍領銜悅活跑團，深度合作垂直媒體與達人實現品牌傳播與品類教育。

conducted a research visit to COFCO Zhishang.

4月23日，中糧集團黨組書記、董事長李國強調研中糧智尚。

8   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2025
         MAJOR EVENTS IN 2025 2025年大事記




On 20 October, China Foods achieved the
industry’s first closed-loop commercialization
of data assets in the food and beverage
industry, becoming the first state-owned
enterprise in this field to realize data
assetization. This breakthrough establishes
a new paradigm for traditional enterprises
exploring the conversion of data value.

10月20日，中國食品首次實現食品飲料行
業數據資產商業化閉環，成為該領域首家數
據資產化央企，為傳統企業探索數據價值轉
化提供了新範式。




On 17 December, the “COFCO Premium
Club” major membership system launched,
integrating private and public domain multi-
channel membership pathways. Following
consolidation, the total membership across
all channels exceeded 81 million, with order
data surpassing 100 million. The Yuexiang
Club achieved unified management of its four
core systems – membership, tiers, points,
and benefits – establishing an integrated
membership operational system.

12 月 17 日 ，「 中 糧 良 品 會 」大 會 員 體 系 上
線，打通私域公域多渠道會員鏈路。整合後
全渠會員超8,100萬，訂單數據破1億單，悅
享會實現會籍、等級、積分、權益四大體系
統一管理，構建一體化會員運營體系。

2025: UPHOLDING HIGH-QUALITY                                            2025年：堅持高質量發展，推

DIMENSIONAL COLLABORATION, AND
ACHIEVING STEADY OPERATIONAL
IMPROVEMENT

In 2025, China Foods anchored itself to the goal of high-quality        2025年，中國食品錨定高質量發展目標，
development, making in-depth efforts across multiple dimensions         在產品品類、營銷網絡、市場執行、供應鏈
including product categories, marketing networks, market execution,     升級、全價值鏈成本管控、數智化賦能及創
supply chain upgrades, full-value-chain cost control, digital and       新業務等多維度縱深發力，各項經營工作取
intelligent empowerment, and innovative businesses. Substantial         得扎實成效，為企業可持續發展築牢根基。

Amidst domestic economic pressures, the nation has forged ahead         內經濟頂壓前行、向新向優，全國居民消費
towards innovation and excellence, with consumer demand steadily        需求穩步釋放，非酒精即飲行業總體保持穩
expanding. The non-alcoholic ready-to-drink sector maintained           定發展態勢。公司精準把握市場趨勢，通過
overall stable development. The Company adeptly navigated market        優化價格體系、升級產品結構、拓展渠道資
trends by optimising its pricing structure, upgrading product           源，實現銷量與收入的量價協同增長，核心
portfolios, and expanding channel resources. This approach              經營指標表現良好。
achieved synergistic growth in both sales volume and revenue, with
core operational metrics demonstrating robust performance.

                                                                              企業管治報告



China Foods Limited (the “Company” and together with its               中國食品有限公司（「本公司」連同其附屬公
subsidiaries, the “Group”) is committed to achieving and maintaining   司統稱「本集團」）致力達成及維持高水平的
high standards of corporate governance to safeguarding the interests   企業管治，以保障股東、投資者、客戶及
of our shareholders, investors, customers and staff, and enhance its   員工之利益及提升其企業價值及問責能力。
corporate value and accountability. Directors of the Company (the      本公司董事（「董事」）深知將良好的企業管治
“Director(s)”) recognize the importance of incorporating elements of   元素納入本集團之管理架構、內部控制及風
good corporate governance in the management structure, internal        險管理程序的重要性從而達致有效的問責機
control and risk management procedures of the Group so as to           制。
achieve effective accountability.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                         企業管治常規

The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on              本公司的企業管治常規乃根據香港聯合交易
the principles and code provisions (the “Code Provisions”) of the      所有限公司證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄
Corporate Governance Code (the “CG Code”) as set out in Appendix       C1《企業管治守則》
                                                                                （「企業管治守則」）所載的
C1 of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock       原則及守則條文（「守則條文」）為基準。
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”).

The Board acknowledges its responsibility for maintaining                 董事會確認其對維持適合及有效的風險管理
appropriate and effective risk management and internal control            和內部監控制度承擔責任，並通過董事會審
systems, and for reviewing the effectiveness of these systems             核委員會檢討這些系統的有效性。
through the audit committee of the Board.

Sound risk management and internal control systems are based on           健全的風險管理和內部監控體系是基於本公
the Company’s good business ethics and culture, the quality and           司良好的商業道德與文化、員工的素質與能
capabilities of its employees, the direction of the Board, and also the   力、董事會的方向以及管理層的效率與能
efficiency and capabilities of management.                                力。

Management of the Company, under the Board supervision, is                本公司的管理層在董事會的監督下，負責風
responsible for the design, implementation and monitoring of the          險管理和內部監控系統的設計，實施和監
risk management and internal control systems.                             督。

The Group has established a sound and comprehensive risk                  本集團已經建立起完善的全面風險管理和內
management and internal control system, formed a systematic and           部監控體系，形成了科學有效的職責分工體
effective system on segregation of duty and three lines of defense in     系，建立了風險管理三道防線，即業務部門
risk management, with each of the business units as the first line of     為第一道防線；各級業務監督檢查部門為第
defense; the business supervision and inspection departments at all       二道防線；內審部和監察部為第三道防線。
levels as the second line of defense; and internal audit department
and supervision department as the third line of defense.

systems based on the COSO (Committee of Sponsoring Organisation           Organisations of the Treadway Commission)
of the Treadway Commission) risk management framework.                    的風險管理框架搭建風險管理及內部監控系

risks.                                                                    持續監督。

comprehensive risk assessment every two years in combination with         營目標，以及市場環境的變化，本集團每兩
the Group’s strategic and business objectives and changes in the          年實施一次全面風險評估。識別對實現戰略
market environment. Identify major risks that adversely affect the        目標造成不利影響的主要風險，並根據一套
achievement of strategic objectives and prioritise the identified risks   標準準則評估及排列所識別風險的優先次
based on a set of standard criteria and formulate a risk mitigation       序，制定風險緩解計劃以便將風險監控至可
plan to monitor the risks to an acceptable level.                         接受水平。

               HKFRS 18 “Presentation and Disclosure in Financial               香港財務報告準則第 18號「財務

               HKFRS 18, which sets out requirements on presentation            香港財務報告準則第18號載列財

               new HKFRS Accounting Standard, while carrying                    表的列報」。此項新的香港財務
               forward many of the requirements in HKAS 1, introduces           報告準則會計準則雖然延續了香
               new requirements to present specified categories and             港會計準則第1號的多項規定，

               alone price of the non-lease components, including                    租賃成分，包括包含租賃土地及
               contract for acquisition of ownership interests of a                  非租賃建築成分的物業所有權權

        Revenue recognition                                                  收入確認
        The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a                          於（或就此）完成履約義務時，本
        performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control”             集團確認收入，即於特定履約義
        of the goods or services underlying the particular                   務的相關商品或服務的「控制權」
        performance obligation is transferred to the customer.               轉讓予客戶時確認。

        A performance obligation represents a good or service (or           履約義務指不同的商品及服務
        a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series         （或一組商品或服務）或一系列大
        of distinct goods or services that are substantially the            致相等的不同商品及服務。
        same.

        Control is transferred over time and revenue is                      控制權隨時間轉移，而倘滿足以
        recognised over time by reference to the progress towards            下其中一項標準，則收入乃參照
        complete satisfaction of the relevant performance                    完全滿足相關履約義務的進展情
        obligation if one of the following criteria is met:                  況而隨時間確認：

        •     the customer simultaneously receives and                       •    於本集團履約時，客戶同
              consumes the benefits provided by the Group’s                       時取得並耗用本集團履約
              performance as the Group performs;                                  所提供的利益；

        •     the Group’s performance creates or enhances                    •    本集團之履約產生或強化
              an asset that the customer controls as the Group                    一項資產，該資產於本集
              performs; or                                                        團履約時乃由客戶控制；
                                                                                  或

        •     the Group’s performance does not create an                     •    本集團的履約並未產生對
              asset with an alternative use to the Group and the                  本集團有替代用途的資
              Group has an enforceable right to payment for                       產，且本集團對迄今已完
              performance completed to date.                                      成履約之付款具有可執行
                                                                                  之權利。

        Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when             否則，收入於客戶獲得商品或服
        the customer obtains control of the distinct goods or                務控制權的時間點確認。
        services.

                                                                                    算。

               Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                  遞延稅項乃按綜合財務報表內資

               of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally            課稅暫時差額確認。遞延稅項資
               recognised for all taxable temporary differences.                    產一般於可能有應課稅溢利用以

               is probable that taxable profits will be available against           時差額自不影響應課稅溢利或會
               which those deductible temporary differences can be                  計溢利之交易之資產及負債初步
               utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not           確認（業務合併除外）所產生，且
               recognised if the temporary difference arises from the               於交易發生時不產生相等的應課
               initial recognition (other than in a business combination)           稅及可扣稅暫時差額，則不會確
               of assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects              認有關遞延稅項資產及負債。此
               neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit                 外，倘暫時差額乃於商譽之初步

               equal taxable and deductible temporary differences. In               確認。

        Intangible assets with finite useful lives that are acquired          單獨取得的使用年期確定的無形
        separately are carried at costs less accumulated                      資產，應按成本減去累計攤銷額
        amortisation and any accumulated impairment losses.                   和累計減值虧損後的餘額列示。

        are recognised separately from goodwill and are initially             開確認之無形資產按其在購買日
        recognised at their fair value at the acquisition date                的公平值（即被視為該等無形資
        (which is regarded as their cost).                                    產的成本）進行初始確認。

        Amortisation for intangible assets with finite useful lives           攤銷額在無形資產的固定使用年
        is recognised on a straight-line basis over their estimated           期內按直線法確認。預計使用壽
        useful lives. The estimated useful life and amortisation              命及攤銷方法會在每一報告期末
        method are reviewed at the end of each reporting                      進行覆核，並採用未來適用法對
        period, with the effect of any changes in estimate being              估計變更的影響進行核算。
        accounted for on a prospective basis.

        Intangible assets with indefinite useful lives that                   單獨取得的使用年期不確定的無
        are acquired separately are carried at cost less any                  形資產，應按成本減去任何其後
        subsequent accumulated impairment losses.                             累計減值虧損後的餘額列示。

        An intangible asset is derecognised on disposal, or when              無形資產在處置時，或在使用或
        no future economic benefits are expected from use or                  處置時預計不會產生經濟利益
        disposal. Gains and losses arising from derecognition of              時，會進行核銷。無形資產在核
        an intangible asset, measured as the difference between               銷時產生的利得或損失由計量處

        is derecognised.                                                      入資產核銷的當期損益。

        Inventories                                                          存貨
        Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net                  存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中

        make the sale.

        Provisions                                                           撥備

        obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of a past             法律或推定現有責任，而預期本
        event, it is probable that the Group will be required to             集團需要清償責任及可以作出可
        settle that obligation, and a reliable estimate can be               靠估計責任之金額時，則需確認
        made of the amount of the obligation.                                撥備。

        of the consideration required to settle the present                  對清償現有責任所需之代價的最
        obligation at the end of the reporting period, taking                佳估計，並考慮責任相關之風險
        into account the risks and uncertainties surrounding                 及不穩定因素。倘撥備是按估計
        the obligation. When a provision is measured using the               清償現有責任之現金流確認的，
        cash flows estimated to settle the present obligation, its           其賬面值即為該現金流量之現
        carrying amount is the present value of those cash flows             時價值（如金錢之時間值為重大
        (when the effect of the time value of money is material).            的）。

               within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessments            告日期後12個月內可能發生的違
               are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss              約事件導致之全期預期信貸虧損
               experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to             部分。評估乃根據本集團的歷史
               the debtors, general economic conditions and an                   信貸虧損經驗進行，並根據債務
               assessment of past events and current conditions at the           人特有的因素、一般經濟狀況以
               reporting date as well as the forecast of future economic         及對過往事件的評估以及對未來
               conditions.                                                       經濟狀況的預測作出調整。

               The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                本集團始終就應收貿易款項確認
               receivables.                                                      全期預期信貸虧損。

               loss allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has                 相等於12個月預期信貸虧損的金
               been a significant increase in credit risk since initial          額計量虧損撥備，除非自初步確

               ECL. The assessment of whether lifetime ECL should                此情況下，本集團確認全期預期

               likelihood or risk of a default occurring since initial           預期信貸虧損乃根據自初步確認

                   past due event;                                                       逾期事件；

                   or contractual reasons relating to the                                與借款人財務困難有
                   borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted                       關之經濟或合約理

                   lender(s) would not otherwise consider;                               款人於其他情況下不

                    severe financial difficulty and there is no realistic                實際收回的可能之時（如交
                    prospect of recovery, for example, when the                          易對手方已進行清盤或進
                    counterparty has been placed under liquidation                       入破產程序），本集團則撇
                    or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings.                          銷金融資產。已撇銷之金
                    Financial assets written off may still be subject to                 融資產仍根據本集團的收
                    enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery                    回程序執行活動，並在適
                    procedures, taking into account legal advice where                   當情況下考慮法律意見。
                    appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition                 撇銷構成終止確認事件。
                    event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised                      其後的任何收回款項均於
                    in profit or loss.                                                   損益中確認。

               (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                             (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確
                                                                                          認
                     The measurement of ECL is a function of the                          預期信貸虧損之計量為違
                     probability of default, loss given default (i.e.                     約概率、違約虧損（即違約
                     the magnitude of the loss if there is a default)                     時虧損大小）及違約時風險
                     and the exposure at default. The assessment                          敞口之函數。違約概率及
                     of the probability of default and loss given                         違約虧損之評估乃基於過
                     default is based on historical data and forward-                     往數據及前瞻性資料。預
                     looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects                      期信貸虧損之估計反映無
                     an unbiased and probability-weighted amount                          偏概率加權平均金額，此
                     that is determined with the respective risks of                      乃以發生違約之相關風險
                     default occurring as the weights. The Group uses                     作為權重而釐定。本集團
                     a practical expedient in estimating ECL on trade                     在估計貿易應收款項的預
                     receivables using a provision matrix taking into                     期信用虧損時，使用實用
                     consideration historical credit loss experience,                     權宜方法，利用一項撥備
                     adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,               矩陣，該矩陣考量歷史信
                     general economic conditions and forward-looking                      用虧損經驗，並根據債務
                     information, including time value of money where                     人的特定因素、整體經濟
                     appropriate, that is available without undue cost or                 狀況及前瞻性資訊（包括在
                     effort.                                                              適當情況下考慮貨幣時間
                                                                                          價值）進行調整，且該等資
                                                                                          訊無須付出不必要成本或
                                                                                          努力即可獲得。

   The Group’s employees in Hong Kong participates in both a            本集團於香港的僱員參與根據職業退
   defined contribution scheme which is registered under the            休計劃條例註冊之定額供款計劃（「職

   Schemes Ordinance in Hong Kong. The assets of the schemes            等計劃之資產乃由受託人控制之基金
   were held, separately from those of the Group, in funds under        持有，與本集團之資產分開處理。
   the control of trustees.

   the statutory limits prescribed by the MPF Ordinance. The            公積金條例規定的法定限額作出。職
   ORSO Scheme is funded by monthly contributions from both             業退休計劃條例計劃的資金來源為僱
   employees and the Group at rates ranging from 5% to 10%              員及本集團每月繳付的供款，供款率
   of the employee’s basic salary, depending on the length of           介乎僱員基本薪金的5%至10%之間，
   service with the Group. Where there were employees who leave         具體取決於僱員在本集團的服務年
   the ORSO Scheme prior to vesting fully in the contributions,         資。若僱員在供款完全歸屬前退出職
   the contributions payable by the Group were reduced by the           業退休計劃條例計劃，本集團應付的
   amount of forfeited contributions. No forfeited contribution was     供款將扣除已沒收供款的金額。於呈
   utilised in this manner during the years presented. At the end       報年度內，並無以此方式動用任何沒
   of the reporting periods, no forfeited contribution, which arose     收供款。於各報告期末，並無因僱員
   upon employees leaving the ORSO Scheme were available to             退出職業退休計劃條例計劃而產生的
   reduce the contributions payable in future years.                    沒收供款，可用作抵減未來年度應付

35 CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           35 資本風險管理

   The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                  本集團管理資本之目標為保障本集團
   safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern         持續經營之能力，以為股東帶來回報
   in order to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for        及為其他權益持有人帶來利益，以及
   other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure      維持最佳資本結構以減低資本成本。
   to reduce the cost of capital. The Group’s overall strategy          本集團的整體策略與去年相比保持不
   remains unchanged from prior year.                                   變。

   In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group      為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能
   may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders,             會調整支付予股東的股息金額、為股
   provide returns for shareholders, issue new shares or sell           東提供回報、發行新股或出售資產。
   assets.

   The Directors review the capital structure on regular basis. As      董事定期檢討資本結構。作為此次檢
   part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital      討的一部分，董事會考慮資本成本及
   and the risks associated with each class of capital. Based on        與各類資本相關的風險。根據董事的
   recommendations of the Directors, the Group will balance its         建議，本集團將透過支付股息、發行
   overall capital structure through the payment of dividends,          新債務或贖回現有債務平衡其整體資
   issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.                本結構。
